/**
 * Returns the credentials the vault holds, or nothing when they cannot be read.
 *
 * <p>The distinction {@link #credentials()} cannot make. An empty map there means "there is no
 * vault", "it is locked" and "it cannot be decrypted" as well as "it is empty", and anything
 * reporting the store's state has to tell the last from the others: "we cannot tell you until
 * you unlock it" and "it is not there" are different sentences, and only one of them is
 * somebody's problem to fix before starting a task.
 *
 * <p>A vault that does not exist reads as an empty set rather than as unreadable. There is
 * nothing to unlock, and telling somebody to unlock it is the one instruction that cannot help
 * them.

     * @return The file, which need not exist.
     */
    public Path defaultProjectFile(String name) {
        return paths.xdg().config().resolve("projects").resolve(name).resolve("project.yml");
    }

private static void deleteTree(final Path root) throws IOException {
    if (!Files.exists(root, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) return;
    // Walked without following a link, so a link the agent left is removed, never its target.
    try (Stream<Path> tree = Files.walk(root)) {
        for (final Path path : tree.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).toList()) {
            Files.deleteIfExists(path);
        }
    }

* Brings the mirror's branches up to the upstream's, before a task starts from them.
*
* <p><strong>A mirror is cloned from the upstream once, and approved work goes back to the

* before it, and its own forward was then refused as no fast-forward (found walking a real
* repository, 2026-10-01). The branches are taken as the upstream has them; what tasks pushed

        final @Nullable Run run, final String issueId) {
    return run == null
            ? java.util.Optional.empty()
            : run.offers().stream()
                    .filter(

                new StoreException.Unreachable(
                        failure.subject(), "The repository could not be reached.");
        case "WorkflowMissing" -> new StoreException.WorkflowMissing(failure.subject());
        default ->
                new StoreException.Unreadable(
                        failure.subject(), "The repository could not be read.");
    };
}

/** The monitor the store swaps its board under, held by a test to stop a swap at its door. */
static Object holding(final GitHubStore store) throws ReflectiveOperationException {
    final java.lang.reflect.Field field = GitHubStore.class.getDeclaredField("holding");
    field.setAccessible(true);
    return Objects.requireNonNull(field.get(store));
}

/// [bytes] as text: cut at [_mostSaid] or not the system's encoding, it is read as UTF-8 with what is broken replaced.
String _decoded(List<int> bytes) {
  try {
    return systemEncoding.decode(bytes);
  } on FormatException {
    return utf8.decode(bytes, allowMalformed: true);
  }
}

/// What ssh said, in words a person can act on.
///
/// A machine that closes a new connection before login says only `Connection closed by <address>` or

/// wait, and does not say which it was. Only those lines, whole: a line naming another fault, such as a
/// banner that is no ssh's, stays ssh's own. Null stays null.

/// How a task's work ended, as Porelo answers it on the task's row: the task's own declaration (`DONE`, or `BLOCKED`
/// with its reason), else `FAILED` with the run's error.
///
/// [outcome] stays Porelo's word, so one this build does not know is passed on, never dropped or mapped.
class TaskEnd {
  /// Constructor taking every field.
  const TaskEnd({required this.outcome, this.why = '', this.at = ''});

/// How a vault entry's name appears inside a task, after `PORELO_TOKEN_` and `PORELO_URL_`:
/// upper-cased, anything but a letter or digit as `_`. Measured: `f56-api` is `PORELO_TOKEN_F56_API`.
static String variableOf(String entry) => entry.toUpperCase().replaceAll(RegExp('[^A-Z0-9]'), '_');

/// Usage: the upstream was asked about the repository {'payments-api'}

/// Usage: the work {'porelo-checkout-shell'} is not shown as {'idle'}
Future<void> theWorkIsNotShownAs(WidgetTester tester, String work, String words) async {
  // A state that is silently wrong is worse than one that says it does not know, so what cannot
  // be seen must not borrow the words for something that can.
  expect(whatItSaysAbout(tester, work), isNot(contains(words)));
}

# Saves the local Maven repository under the run's key when the job fetched a jar or pom the restored cache lacked,
# and only then: a run that fetched nothing saves no copy that would only crowd out the other caches.
name: Maven cache, saved
description: Saves ~/.m2/repository under the given key when the job added a jar or pom after the cache was restored.
inputs:
    key:
        description: The key the pinned-jdk action restored under, its output maven-cache-key.
        required: true

    using: composite
    steps:

echo "nothing fetched after the restore: nothing saved"

# and build/pom.xml alone does not say what the build tools' snapshot resolves to.
- uses: actions/cache/restore@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0
  id: maven-cache
  with:

      key: maven-${{ runner.os }}-${{ hashFiles('build/pom.xml') }}-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
      restore-keys: |

[[ $sha256 =~ ^[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]] || { echo "::error::porelo-machines named no pinned digest"; exit 1; }
echo "sha256=$sha256" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

# A reboot of a host set up by up, with the ordering Scaleway's Ubuntu image has and the test image lacks: cloud-init's
# network stage after networkd's wait-online and before sysinit.target. A firewall unit after sysinit.target and before
# network-pre.target closes a cycle with it (networkd starts after network-pre.target); systemd then drops a job to
# break it, which on Scaleway left the boot hanging, and here at least says so in the journal.
# Afterwards the host is as down expects it: the homeserver and the forge answering again under linger.
- name: Reboot the host with Scaleway's ordering, and always take that ordering back
  block:
    - name: Make the drop-in directory of cloud-init's network stage

# The GraalVM the CI snapshots pin, installed on a GitHub runner and checked against its digest - so a

# key is never saved over, and the poms alone do not say what they resolve to.

# The wrapper's Maven, fetched once per pin: without it every job fetched it with one wget and no retry, and a
# single network hiccup failed a Build (2026-11-06). Saved once the wrapper ran, below.
- uses: actions/cache/restore@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0

run: |
    set -eu
    . "$GITHUB_ACTION_PATH/pinned.sh"
    VERSION=3.12.0
    pinned_fetch "https://github.com/mvdan/sh/releases/download/v$VERSION/shfmt_v${VERSION}_linux_amd64" \
        d9fbb2a9c33d13f47e7618cf362a914d029d02a6df124064fff04fd688a745ea \
        "$HOME/.cache/porelo-pinned/shfmt/shfmt"
    mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/bin"
    install -m 0755 "$HOME/.cache/porelo-pinned/shfmt/shfmt" "$RUNNER_TEMP/bin/shfmt"

    || { echo "::error::porelo-machines named no pinned digest"; exit 1; }

strategy:
    fail-fast: false
    matrix:
        os: [ubuntu, fedora]
steps:
    # A forge with no registry or no token for it would quietly read Sonatype's snapshots alone.
    - name: A forge names its registry and its token
      env:
          SERVER_URL: ${{ github.server_url }}
      run: |
          [ "$SERVER_URL" != 'https://github.com' ] || exit 0

# Proven by building the product on it before it is taken. On a push only when the newest snapshot was
# made from other inputs - the recipe with its pins, the toolchain, the code that runs them - which the
# command names and asks before it rents; started by hand it always builds, the way to recover.
- name: Build the ${{ matrix.os }} snapshot
  timeout-minutes: 60
  env:

needs: [build]
if: >-
    github.event_name == 'push'
    && (github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' || startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v'))

# A release depends only on releases: every snapshot in the effective pom - a dependency, a plugin, a plugin's
# dependency - refused and named, by the porelo-release this very commit builds, installed first.
- name: No snapshot in a release

-Dmdep.outputFile=target/mirror-cp.txt

# Nothing for the workflow as a whole: the one permission this needs is given to the one job that deletes.

total=$(wc -l < all.tsv)

# A run still in progress answers 409 rather than deleting; that is a warning, not a
# failure, because the rest of the sweep is still worth doing.

# Never two at once: two refreshes would open the same pull request.
concurrency:
    group: ${{ github.workflow }}

env:
    GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
run: |
    set -u
    release() {
        java -cp "release/target/classes:$(cat release/target/cp.txt)" \
            org.fuin.porelo.release.Main "$@"
    }
    git config user.name 'github-actions[bot]'
    git config user.email '41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com'
    # One branch, rebuilt from main on every run, one commit per moved pin: a merge rebuilds the

title="Pin $moved"

    gh pr edit "$branch" --title "$title" --body "$body"

#lang shell

# shellcheck shell=bash
# The one rule by which a pinned download is tried again, sourced by the steps of this action and by any script that
# fetches something pinned (shfmt, the wrapper's Maven, fd, ripgrep, Node): the same numbers as porelo-pinned-rule's
# PinnedRetry, which a test in porelo-machines holds this file to. The pauses double from 15 s, none longer than 5 min,
# until 15 min of pauses are spent, the last one cut to fit: 15 30 60 120 240 300 135, eight attempts in all. GitHub's

# after every fetch and before it is taken from a cache. What the functions say goes to standard error.
# A file the first source does not serve - a 5xx or no answer past 600 s, a 4xx, anything but a wrong digest - is
# fetched from the organization's Artifactory, pinned_mirror, by its digest and name:
# <mirror>/pinned/<sha256>/<file name>, without a token, until the 900 s are spent, and taken only when its digest
# matches. PORELO_PINNED_MIRROR names another mirror, an https address, or none when it is set empty; without a mirror
# the first source has the whole 900 s. An attempt runs no longer than its source's deadline leaves, at least 30 s.
# On GitHub, a download from https://github.com goes with the job's token, PORELO_PINNED_GITHUB_TOKEN, so it counts
# against the job's own limit: only when GITHUB_SERVER_URL is exactly https://github.com, the address is on it and the
# token has a token's form. The token is handed to curl on its standard input, never as an argument, and goes with the
# address asked for alone: the address it redirects to is fetched without it. A 401 or 403 is not tried again.

pinned_now() { # whole seconds on a clock that only counts up; a test replaces it
    local up
    read -r up _ </proc/uptime || return 1
    echo "${up%%.*}"
}

left=$((deadline - elapsed))
[ "$1" -ge 1 ] && [ "$1" -lt "$pinned_attempts" ] && [ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 1
while [ "$n" -lt "$1" ]; do

pinned_fetch() { # <url> <sha256> <file>: the file the url serves, checked, or the mirror's copy of it when the url

    return 1
fi
# A link is no cached file: it would hand over whatever it points at.
if [ -f "$file" ] && [ ! -L "$file" ] && [ "$(pinned_sha256 "$file")" = "$sha256" ]; then
    echo "$file taken from the cache, checked against $sha256" >&2
    return 0
fi
if [ -n "$mirror" ]; then

mkdir -p "$(dirname "$file")" || return 1
start=$(pinned_now) || return 1
if [ -z "$second" ]; then
    pinned_from only "$url" "$sha256" "$file" "$start" || status=$?
    [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || return 1
    return 0
fi
pinned_from first "$url" "$sha256" "$file" "$start" || status=$?
# 1 is a fault the mirror must not cover, such as a wrong digest; 2 is a source that did not serve the file.
[ "$status" -eq 2 ] || return "$status"

esac
while :; do
    # An attempt ends by its source's deadline, with 30 s for one that starts on it.
    elapsed=$(($(pinned_now) - start))
    most=$((deadline - elapsed))
    [ "$most" -ge 30 ] || most=30
    [ "$most" -le 600 ] || most=600
    part=$(mktemp "$file.XXXXXX") || return 1

-w '%{http_code} %{redirect_url}' "$url") || exit=$?

        -o "$part" -w '%{http_code}' "$next") || exit=$?
fi
case $exit:$code in
    0:200)
        if [ "$(pinned_sha256 "$part")" != "$sha256" ]; then
            rm -f "$part"
            echo "::error::$url has the digest of something else, not the pinned $sha256" >&2

            return 2
        fi
        echo "::warning::$url answered HTTP ${code:-000} (curl exit $exit) on attempt $attempt of $pinned_attempts;" \
            "trying again in $wait s" >&2
        sleep "$wait"
        attempt=$((attempt + 1))
    done
}

pinned_retry() { # <what> <command>...: runs the command until it succeeds
    local what=$1 attempt=1 wait start elapsed status errexit=

status=$?
[ -z "$errexit" ] || set -e
[ "$status" -ne 0 ] || return 0
elapsed=$(($(pinned_now) - start))
if ! wait=$(pinned_pause "$attempt" "$elapsed"); then
    echo "::error::$what failed; given up after $attempt of at most $pinned_attempts attempts in" \
        "$elapsed s" >&2
    return 1

    exit 1
fi
hidden=$(printf '%s\n' "$listing" | grep -E '(^|/)\.' || true)
if [ -n "$hidden" ]; then
    echo "REFUSED: $1 holds a dot file, which is a machine's or a tool's, never the collection's:" >&2

#!/bin/sh
# Fetches the porelo-host package the role holds the host to, its floor, and porelo of the same run, from Porelo's repository, for a Molecule
# run with no local hand-over (GitHub's runner): both come from one porelo build, as a local hand-over does. apt takes

#                                                    PORELO_HOST_PACKAGES the directory

trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
curl -fsSL --proto '=https' -o "$work/key.asc" "$key_url" || fail "cannot fetch Porelo's key from $key_url"
found=$(gpg --batch --with-colons --show-keys "$work/key.asc" | awk -F: '$1 == "fpr" { print $10 }')
[ "$found" = "$fingerprint" ] || fail "the key at $key_url is not the one with the fingerprint $fingerprint"
gpg --batch --dearmor -o "$work/porelo.gpg" "$work/key.asc"
mkdir -p "$work/lists/partial" "$work/cache/archives/partial" "$work/etc/preferences.d" "$work/etc/sources.list.d"
printf 'deb [signed-by=%s arch=amd64] %s %s main\n' "$work/porelo.gpg" "$url" "$distribution" > "$work/etc/sources.list"
: > "$work/status"
set -- -q -o Dir::Etc="$work/etc" -o Dir::State::Lists="$work/lists" -o Dir::State::status="$work/status" \

#!/bin/sh
# Runs a Molecule scenario against a VM made for this run through libvirt on this machine: Ubuntu 26.04 from the image
# build/test-image pins, nested KVM, a host key made for the run and accepted alone. It deletes exactly that VM, its
# disk and its seed afterwards, after a failure and on an interrupt too, and refuses to start over one an earlier run
# left. The downloaded image and its base volume are kept as a cache, named by their digest.

export LIBVIRT_DEFAULT_URI=qemu:///system

# Ubuntu mirrors the VM's apt tries in this order instead of the image's own, where the caller knows ones that answer;
# they go into a file.
if [ -n "${PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_MIRRORS+set}" ]; then

        named=$((named + 1))
    done
    [ "$named" -gt 0 ] || fail 'PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_MIRRORS must be http or https addresses, at least one'
fi

trap 'rm -rf "$packages_stage"' EXIT

# The VM as a cloud server without virtualization is, so base's check is the real one's other side.
[ "$kvm_check" = on ] || export PORELO_VM_CPU=host-passthrough,disable=vmx,disable=svm
forge_check=${PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_FORGE:-on}
case "$forge_check" in on|off) ;; *) fail 'PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_FORGE must be on or off' ;; esac
[ "$forge_check" = on ] || [ "$pool_check" = off ] \
    || fail 'PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_FORGE=off requires PORELO_HOST_MOLECULE_POOL=off: pool jobs need the forge runner'
machines=
if [ "$pool_check" = on ]; then
    machines=${PORELO_HOST_MACHINES:?set PORELO_HOST_MACHINES to the checked runtime JAR directory}
    machines_digest=${PORELO_HOST_MACHINES_SHA256:?set PORELO_HOST_MACHINES_SHA256 to its manifest SHA-256}
    printf '%s\n' "$machines_digest" | grep -Eqx '[0-9a-f]{64}' || fail 'no SHA-256 for the tooling manifest'
    [ "$(sha256sum "$machines/SHA256SUMS" | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$machines_digest" ] \

    for file in ./*.jar revision; do
        [ -f "$file" ] && [ ! -L "$file" ] || fail "no regular tooling input: $file"
        [ "$(awk -v name="${file#./}" '$2 == name {n++} END {print n+0}' SHA256SUMS)" = 1 ] \
            || fail "the tooling manifest must cover $file exactly once"
    done
    sha256sum --check --strict SHA256SUMS || exit 1
    grep -Eqx '[0-9a-f]{40}' revision || fail 'no source commit in tooling revision'
) || fail 'the acceptance tooling did not pass its digest checks'
say "pool acceptance tooling from source commit $(cat "$machines/revision")"

# --- clean up exactly what this run made ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

trap 'exit 130' INT TERM

# Ubuntu's sums are read only for a download: a cached image is held to its pinned digest below.

    say "downloading ${url##*/} once, about 865 MB"
    curl -fsSL --retry 3 -o "$image.part" "$url" || fail "could not download $url"
    mv "$image.part" "$image"
fi
[ "$(sha256sum "$image" | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$digest" ] || fail "$image does not have the digest $digest"
base=porelo-host-base-$(printf '%s' "$digest" | cut -c1-12).qcow2
"$vm" base "$image" "$digest" "$base" >/dev/null

until vm true 2>/dev/null; do
    [ $(($(date +%s) - started)) -lt $limit ] \

done
# Waited for once: anything but a clean finish - a schema warning too - is a fault of the seed, and waiting longer
# would not mend it.

# --- the scenario -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
work=/work/ansible_collections/porelo/host
"$root/build/tools.sh" ansible-galaxy collection build --output-path "target/molecule/$run" >/dev/null
version=$(sed -n 's/^version: *//p' "$root/galaxy.yml")
cat > "$dir/inventory.yml" <<EOF
all:

#!/bin/sh
# The check of an agent's move, read-only, run on the host in the account of the person who started the task. Each line
# says what it found and fails by name, so it is red before a trial and green after. It posts nothing: whoever posted
# the waking message in the room says they read its answer there, with --wake-answered.
# Each line reads its fact where it is held. The push: the head on the host's forge of the task's branch from
# `porelo task status`, or the next free one the gate's approve takes
# (<branch>-2, -3, ...), in the one repository of the project's organization that holds it, since the forge names a
# repository by its central name and the task by its key; read through the forge's git port with the person's vault
# answering Git's credential protocol, so no token is on a command line and none is made anew. The Build: `porelo
# build status` for that branch; the host names builds: for every project on its forge, so "not configured" fails.
# The conversation: a message the task sent that the matrix transport took, from `porelo talk log`. The pull request:
# the forge's open requests for that head and the repository's default branch.

set -u
[ $# -ge 2 ] || {
    echo "usage: $0 <task> <agent's name> [--wake-answered]" >&2
    exit 64
}

field() { # <field>, from the JSON on standard input; an absent or null one prints nothing

    value = None
print("" if value is None else str(value).lower() if isinstance(value, bool) else value)' "$1"
}

line 1 "the host program: expected porelo-host $expected_host_version, got $host_version (exit $host_version_status)"

api() { # <path under /api/v1>: the forge's answer when it is 200, else what was answered; the token on curl's config
    credential=$(printf 'protocol=http\nhost=127.0.0.1:%s\n\n' "$port" \
        | porelo vault credential --entry "$forge.person" 2>/dev/null) || {
        echo "the vault gave no token for $forge.person"
        return 1

}
answer=$(printf '%s\n' "$config" | curl -q -sS --max-redirs 0 --noproxy '*' --proto '=http' \
    --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 30 -K - -w '\n%{http_code}' "$base/api/v1/$1" 2>/dev/null) || {
    echo "the host's forge gave no answer for ${1%%\?*}"
    return 1
}
code=${answer##*"$newline"}
if [ "$code" != 200 ]; then
    echo "the host's forge answered HTTP $code for ${1%%\?*}"
    return 1
fi

repositories() { # the names of the organization's repositories on the forge, one a line, each held to its form
    page=1
    while :; do
        answer=$(api "orgs/$project/repos?limit=50&page=$page") || {
            printf '%s\n' "$answer"
            return 1
        }

        END { if (found != "") { sub(/^refs\/heads\//, "", found); print found } }'
}

names=$(repositories) || listed=$?
case $listed in
    0) ;;
    1) why=$(printf '%s\n' "$names" | tail -n 1) ;;
    2) why="the host's forge answered an invalid list of the repositories of $project" ;;
    *) why="the host's forge answered more than 50 pages of repositories of $project" ;;
esac
holders=
if [ "$listed" = 0 ]; then
    for name in $names; do

holder=$name
heads=$answer

    held=
fi
if [ -n "$held" ]; then

else
    case $why in
        "no repository "* | "several repositories "*) line 1 "its last push reached the forge: $why" ;;
        *) line 1 "its last push reached the forge: cannot say: $why" ;;
    esac
fi

elif [ "$asked" -eq 0 ]; then
    verdict=$(printf '%s' "$build" | field verdict)
    case $verdict in success | failure | running | queued | cancelled) found=0 ;; esac
    # A Build of an earlier commit, or of a branch the push never reached, is not one of that push.
    read_commit=$(printf '%s' "$build" | field commit)
    if [ "$found" = 0 ] && { [ -z "$pushed" ] || [ "$read_commit" != "$pushed" ]; }; then
        verdict="of ${read_commit:-no commit}, not of the push ${pushed:-that was not found}"
        found=1
    fi
fi
line "$found" "a Build of that push was read by the forge's build reader: ${verdict:-nothing}"

requests = json.load(sys.stdin)
if not isinstance(requests, list):
    sys.exit(1)
print("found" if any(p["head"]["ref"] == sys.argv[1] and p["base"]["ref"] == sys.argv[2]
                     for p in requests) else "more" if requests else "end")' "$held" "$default" 2>/dev/null); then
            why="the host's forge answered invalid pull requests"
            break
        fi
        case $match in found) found=0; break ;; end) break ;; esac
        page=$((page + 1))

else
    line 1 "the pull request was opened by the gate: $why"
fi

found=1
[ "$answered" != --wake-answered ] || found=0

#        porelo-vm delete <name>                             the VM, its disk and its seed, believed only as seen
set -eu
export LIBVIRT_DEFAULT_URI="${LIBVIRT_DEFAULT_URI:-qemu:///system}"
# virsh's words are read below, so they must not be translated.
export LC_ALL=C
pool=images

base() {
    image=$1 digest=$2 volume=$3
    name_ok "$volume"
    printf '%s\n' "$digest" | grep -Eqx '[0-9a-f]{64}' || fail "'$digest' is no SHA-256"
    [ "$(sha256sum "$image" | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$digest" ] || fail "$image does not have the digest $digest"
    if ! virsh vol-info "$volume" --pool "$pool" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        virsh vol-create-as "$pool" "$volume" "$(stat -c %s "$image")" --format qcow2 >/dev/null
        if ! virsh vol-upload "$volume" "$image" --pool "$pool"; then
            virsh vol-delete "$volume" --pool "$pool" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

seed() {
    dir=$1 name=$2 account=$3 key=$4 host=$5 part=${6:-}

echo 'ssh_pwauth: false'
echo 'ssh_deletekeys: true'
echo 'ssh_genkeytypes: [ed25519]'
echo 'ssh_keys:'
echo '  ed25519_private: |'
sed 's/^/    /' "$host"
echo "  ed25519_public: \"$(cat "$host.pub")\""

        --network network=default --osinfo "$osinfo" --graphics none --noautoconsole \
        --channel unix,target.type=virtio,target.name=org.qemu.guest_agent.0 >/dev/null
}; then
    rm -f "$seed/seed.iso"
    delete "$name"
    fail "could not make $name ($name.qcow2, $name-seed.iso)"
fi

delete() {
    name=$1
    name_ok "$name"
    # Stopped before it is undefined: undefining a running domain only makes it transient, and it runs on.
    virsh destroy "$name" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    virsh undefine "$name" --snapshots-metadata >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    virsh vol-delete "$name.qcow2" --pool "$pool" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    virsh vol-delete "$name-seed.iso" --pool "$pool" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    # Believed only as seen: what libvirt still lists of it is left behind, whatever the commands answered.

        | grep -Fx -e "$name.qcow2" -e "$name-seed.iso" || true)"
    if [ -n "$(printf '%s' "$kept" | tr -d ' ')" ]; then fail "LEFT BEHIND, delete by hand: $kept"; fi
}

#!/bin/sh
# Rehearses an agent's move before a host is rented: a throwaway VM on this machine's libvirt, made by the same code as
# Molecule's test VM, with `up` run on it from this checkout's collection, the forge on and no pool. The organization is

# organization keeps the host's read key on its repositories: nobody takes it off but the repositories' owner.
# `rented` rehearses check, up and down on an existing server, with a pinned key and a report outside this checkout.

#                                                              check, up and down on a server already rented, with
#                                                              the forge, and a VM pool and job capacity when asked
set -eu
root=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
export LIBVIRT_DEFAULT_URI=qemu:///system
# virsh's words are read below, so they must not be translated.
export LC_ALL=C
pool=images
prefix=porelo-host-rehearsal
state=$root/target/rehearsal

    domains=$(virsh list --all --name) || fail 'could not list libvirt domains'
    volumes=$(virsh vol-list "$pool") || fail 'could not list libvirt volumes'
    printf '%s\n%s\n' "$domains" "$(printf '%s\n' "$volumes" | awk 'NR > 2 {print $1}')" | grep "^$prefix-" || true
}

on_vm() { # <command>..., with standard input passed on; -- keeps a command from reading as ssh's own option
    at=$(address)
    ssh -o BatchMode=yes -o IdentitiesOnly=yes -o ConnectTimeout=5 -i "$state/id_ed25519" \
        -o UserKnownHostsFile="$state/known_hosts" -o StrictHostKeyChecking=yes -- "$account@$at" "$@"
}

start() {
    machine_file='' bare='' cpus=8 memory=16 disk=80
    while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
        if [ "$1" = --bare ]; then
            bare=yes
            shift
            continue
        fi
        [ $# -ge 2 ] || usage
        case $1 in
            --machine-file) machine_file=$2 ;;

name=$prefix-$(date -u +%Y%m%dt%H%M%Sz)
made=
image=
finish() {

    fi
    exit "$status"
}
trap finish EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT TERM

    printf '%s\n' "$sums" | grep -qx "$digest \*${url##*/}" \
        || fail "Ubuntu's SHA256SUMS does not name $digest for ${url##*/}"
    mkdir -p "$cache"
    curl -fsSL --retry 3 -o "$image.part" "$url" || fail "could not download $url"
    mv "$image.part" "$image"
fi
[ "$(sha256sum "$image" | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$digest" ] || fail "$image does not have the digest $digest"
base=porelo-host-base-$(printf '%s' "$digest" | cut -c1-12).qcow2
"$vm" base "$image" "$digest" "$base" >/dev/null

# ubuntu25.10: osinfo-db as Ubuntu 26.04 ships it knows no ubuntu26.04 yet.
PORELO_VM_DISK=${disk}G "$vm" make "$name" "$base" "$state" $((memory * 1024)) "$cpus" ubuntu25.10
started=$(date +%s)
"$vm" address "$name" "$limit" > "$state/address"
printf '%s %s\n' "$(cat "$state/address")" "$(cut -d' ' -f1,2 "$state/host_ed25519.pub")" > "$state/known_hosts"
until on_vm true < /dev/null 2>/dev/null; do
    [ $(($(date +%s) - started)) -lt $limit ] || fail "$name took no ssh login within $limit s"
    sleep 3
done
on_vm cloud-init status --wait < /dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 \
    || fail "cloud-init on $name did not finish cleanly: $(on_vm cloud-init status --long < /dev/null 2>&1)"

        # systemd owns the remote processes even when an interrupted ssh connection has already gone away.

            bash -s "$account" "$given" rented "$kvm" "$staging" "$unit" "${packages_digest:-}" \

            < "$state/packages.tar"

down() {

stop_tree() {
    # Snapshot descendants before TERM can reparent them, then signal the parent before its children.
    stop_pids=''
    collect_tree "$1"
    for stop_pid in $stop_pids; do kill -TERM "$stop_pid" 2>/dev/null || true; done
    attempts=0
    while [ "$attempts" -lt 50 ]; do
        live=no
        for stop_pid in $stop_pids; do

rented_abort_pending() {

        rented_line "FAIL $current finish the step -> $pending_reason"
        current=''

        rented_line "FAIL between steps after ${last_step:-none} -> $pending_reason"
    fi
    exit "$pending_code"

    fi

rented_down() {
    on_rented bash -s "$unit" "$staging" <<'EOF'
set -euo pipefail

    emit_file down.lines /root/rehearsal-down.lines || read_status=$?
    [ "$status" = 0 ] || exit "$status"
    [ "$read_status" = 0 ] || exit "$read_status"
    python3 -I -c 'import json,sys
rows=[json.loads(line) for line in open(sys.argv[1]) if line.strip()]
sys.exit(1 if not rows or any(row.get("state") in ("failed", "waiting") for row in rows) else 0)' /root/rehearsal-down.lines
    # A clean down leaves the software and /etc/porelo-host; this mark lets a later run past the guard on this server.
    printf '%s\n' "$unit" > /root/porelo-rehearsal-down
else
    printf '{"down_ran":false}\n'

rented_save_artifacts() {
    python3 -I -c 'import json,os,pathlib,sys
report=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]); state=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]); ran=None
with open(report/"down.out") as stream:
    for line in stream:
        row=json.loads(line)
        if "file" in row:
            if row["file"] not in ("up.lines", "down.lines") or not isinstance(row["content"], str):
                raise ValueError("unexpected cleanup artifact")

            ran=row["down_ran"]
        else:
            raise ValueError("unexpected cleanup answer")
if ran is None:
    raise ValueError("cleanup did not say whether down ran")
(state/"down-ran").write_text(str(ran).lower())' "$report" "$state"
}

on_person() {
    remote=
    for argument do
        quoted=$(printf '%s' "$argument" | sed "s/'/'\\\\''/g")

# Root through the person's agent, whose key --org-key put among root's keys: the host's own org verbs are root's
# alone and never run through sudo. env -i drops the SSH session's variables, which the host program refuses.
on_root() {
    # The remote shell expands its own PATH.
    # shellcheck disable=SC2016
    remote='exec env -i PATH="$PATH"'
    for argument do
        quoted=$(printf '%s' "$argument" | sed "s/'/'\\\\''/g")
        remote="$remote '$quoted'"

# Root's keys are the file up made; a follow later writes the host admins' keys there, so a key it holds already stays.
rented_root_key() {
    # These positional arguments expand in the remote shell.
    # shellcheck disable=SC2016
    printf '%s\n' "$org_public" | on_rented sh -c 'set -eu
keys=/etc/ssh/authorized_keys/root
key=$(cat)
if [ ! -f "$keys" ] || [ -L "$keys" ]; then echo "up made no root key file at $keys"; exit 1; fi
if awk -v key="$key" '"'"'$1 " " $2 == key { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'"'"' "$keys"; then exit 0; fi
work=$(mktemp "$keys.XXXXXX")
trap '"'"'rm -f -- "$work"'"'"' EXIT
cat "$keys" > "$work"

chmod 0644 "$work"
mv -fT "$work" "$keys"'
}

    on_org carry --id "$change-carry" rented "$project"
}

#!/bin/sh
# Runs one command on the rehearsal's host as the test person, for a step under build/rehearse-steps: through the
# person's agent alone and the host key pinned at the run's first connection. Each argument is quoted for the remote
# shell; standard input is forwarded.

remote=
for argument do

# The remote shell expands its own PATH.
# shellcheck disable=SC2016
remote='exec env -i PATH="$PATH"'

#!/bin/sh
# The busy-room step of porelo-host's rented-host run (build/rehearse-move.sh, build/rehearse-steps/busy-room there): the
# project's room on the host rehearsed as the test person, after the `tasks` step started its tasks. Kept here, in
# porelo-message-matrix, and copied into porelo-host as it is.
# It runs this repository's build/rehearse-busy-room.sh from a fresh clone of main in its own scratch directory, from
# REHEARSAL_MATRIX_SOURCE (a URL, path or bundle; default the public repository over https), and writes in no checkout.
# The project's other active persons come from the host's own answer, <report>/org-status.json, which the run's project
# step wrote as root with `porelo-host org status --json`: their accounts and helpers in the room are no stranger's.
# Every ssh goes as the person alone: the run's agent, the run's pinned host key, no other key. Its exit is the rehearsal's, so 0 only when every scenario held; 77 (SKIP)
# when too little time is left before the hard stop to wait for the tasks' answers.
set -u
for name in REHEARSAL_HOST REHEARSAL_PERSON REHEARSAL_PERSON_AGENT REHEARSAL_KNOWN_HOSTS REHEARSAL_PROJECT \

left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s)))
# Ten scenarios, each waiting up to five minutes for an answer and a minute of quiet: about 20 min when the tasks answer.

set -f
# shellcheck disable=SC2086 # split on purpose
set -- $persons
set +f
echo "busy-room: the project's other persons: ${*:-none}"

    rm -rf "$work"
}
trap end EXIT
trap 'exit 75' INT TERM HUP

# Every ssh the rehearsal opens - the forward and each task's talk log - goes as the person, by the run's agent and key.
mkdir "$work/bin"
cat >"$work/bin/ssh" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
exec /usr/bin/ssh -o "IdentityAgent=$REHEARSAL_PERSON_AGENT" -o IdentityFile=none \

status=$?
echo "busy-room: the rehearsal ended $status"
# 77 is the run's SKIP, but from the rehearsal it is a refusal by the gate or the homeserver: a failure.

left() { echo $((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s))); }
on_person() { # <command> <argument>...: on the host as the person, ended at the hard stop
    [ "$(left)" -gt 0 ] || { echo "the hard stop passed" >&2; return 124; }

print("" if value is None else str(value).lower() if isinstance(value, bool) else value)' "$1"
}
forge_branch() { awk '$1 == "forge" { print $2; exit }'; }

# The commit, made in the task's workspace by podman exec as the person; the container's git signs it at the gate.
commit=$(in_task 'git commit -q --allow-empty -m "rehearsal judge: pushes through the gate" && git rev-parse HEAD') \
    || { echo "no commit could be made in $container"; exit 1; }
printf '%s\n' "$commit" | grep -Eqx '[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64}' || { echo "the commit is not named: $commit"; exit 1; }
echo "committed $commit in $container"

    echo "the task's own push of $commit was refused:"
    tail -n 3 "$report/judge.push.log"
    exit 1
fi
echo "the task's push of $commit through the gate was accepted for $branch"

    echo "the gate let $commit onto $ref"
    exit 1
fi
grep -qF "$refusal" "$report/judge.refused.log" || {
    echo "the push to $ref failed, but not by the gate's refusal:"
    tail -n 3 "$report/judge.refused.log"
    exit 1
}
echo "the gate refused $commit on $ref"

        exit 1
        ;;
esac
[ "$(left)" -gt 20 ] || {

local left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
[ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 124

wait_on() {
    waiting+=("$1")
    echo "expected while $1 is open: $2"
}

    # shellcheck disable=SC2016
    on_host 60 sh -c 'rm -f "${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/porelo/providers/example-unknown.yaml"; rm -rf "$1"' \
        sh "$scratch" < /dev/null > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
}
trap cleanup EXIT

# the key's lines arrive as a paste does, each ended by the Enter the terminal sends.
echo "== vault put, pasted at the prompt"
paste_driver='import os, pty, select, sys, time
name, key = sys.argv[1], open(sys.argv[2], "rb").read()
pid, fd = pty.fork()

            return False
        if not data:
            return False
        seen += data
        if until is not None and until in seen:
            return True
    return False
if not read_for(60, b"Value for"):
    sys.stdout.write(seen.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
    print("\nno prompt for the value within a minute")
    os.kill(pid, 9)
    sys.exit(3)

    print("\nvault put did not end within a minute of the paste")
    sys.exit(4)
sys.stdout.write(seen.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
sys.exit(os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status))'
status=0

# Step 5: a provider Pi does not know. Its file declares a dialect Pi speaks, and its credential is there, so the only
# thing wrong is the provider's name; the start is refused before anything is made, naming it.
echo "== a provider Pi does not know"
# shellcheck disable=SC2016

dir=${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/porelo/providers
mkdir -p "$dir"
cat > "$dir/example-unknown.yaml"' > "$out/provider.out" 2>&1 <<'YAML'; then

  openai: "/v1"

    SHA256:*)
        timeout 60 "$REHEARSAL_ORG" credentials trust-host --fingerprint="$pinned" "$REHEARSAL_HOST" < /dev/null \
            > "$out/trust-host.out" 2>&1 || fail "the host's key" "$(tail -n 1 "$out/trust-host.out")"
        ;;
    *) fail "the host's key" "no fingerprint for the pinned host key" ;;
esac
left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
[ "$left" -lt 600 ] || left=600

shift
local left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
[ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 124
[ "$seconds" -le "$left" ] || seconds=$left

wait_on() {
    [[ " ${waiting[*]} " == *" $1 "* ]] || waiting+=("$1")
    echo "expected while $1 is open: $2"
}

# shellcheck disable=SC2016
on_host 60 sh -c 'cd "$1"
sed -n "/<modules>/,/<\/modules>/p" pom.xml
grep -ohE "case \"[a-z-]+\"" release/src/main/java/org/fuin/porelo/release/Main.java | sort -u
ls -d stats 2>/dev/null || true' sh "$scratch/porelo-buildtools" < /dev/null > "$out/buildtools.out" 2>&1 || true
cat "$out/buildtools.out"
grep -qE '<module>(ffm-check|cpu-check)</module>' "$out/buildtools.out" \

#
# A step under build/rehearse-steps, called by the contract in doc/testing.md; it reads REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH,
# REHEARSAL_REPORT and REHEARSAL_DEADLINE, and PORELO_STORY_PLUGINS, the plugin repository's descriptor (default the

    local left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
    [ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 124
    [ "$seconds" -le "$left" ] || seconds=$left
    timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=10 "$seconds" "$REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH" "$@"
}

fail() {
    [ -n "$failed" ] || failed="$1: $2"
    echo "FAILED $1: $2"
}

# The notices against the package's bill, judged here: every library the bill names that is not Porelo's own is named

if bill is None:
    print("NOTE no bill in the package; the notices are not held to one")
    sys.exit(0)
def own(c):
    return (c.get("group") or "").startswith("org.fuin.porelo") or (c.get("name") or "").startswith("porelo")
def ids(c):
    found = []
    for entry in c.get("licenses", []):
        lic = entry.get("license", {})

    if c.get("type", "library") != "library" or own(c):

        base = re.sub(r"-(only|or-later)$|\+$", "", lid)
        phrase = texts.get(base)
        if phrase and phrase.upper() not in notices.upper():
            print("FAIL " + name + " is " + lid + ", and its text is not in the notices")
    if name.startswith("bc") and "Bouncy Castle" not in notices:
        print("FAIL " + name + " is Bouncy Castle, and its licence is not in the notices")
print("NOTE " + str(named) + " third-party libraries in the bill")'

    dnf download -q --destdir . "$2" >/dev/null
    cd root && rpm2cpio ../*.rpm | cpio -idm --quiet && cd ..
fi
find root -name THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES -type f | sort | sed -n 1p
find root -name "*.cdx.json" -type f | sort | sed -n 1p' sh "$scratch" "$package" "$format" < /dev/null \
        > "$out/$package.found" 2>&1; then
        cat "$out/$package.found"
        fail "the package $package" "it could not be downloaded or unpacked: $(tail -n 1 "$out/$package.found")"
        continue
    fi
    notices=$(grep -m 1 'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES$' "$out/$package.found" || true)
    bill=$(grep -m 1 '\.cdx\.json$' "$out/$package.found" || true)

on_host 60 cat "$scratch/$package/$notices" < /dev/null > "$out/$package.notices" 2> /dev/null || true
args=("$out/$package.notices")
if [ -n "$bill" ] \
    && on_host 60 cat "$scratch/$package/$bill" < /dev/null > "$out/$package.cdx.json" 2> /dev/null; then
    args+=("$out/$package.cdx.json")
fi
if ! python3 -I -c "$judge" "${args[@]}" > "$out/$package.judged" 2>&1; then
    fail "the notices of $package" "they could not be judged: $(tail -n 1 "$out/$package.judged")"

                fail "the notices of $package" "${judged#FAIL }"
            fi
            ;;
    esac
done < "$out/$package.judged"

# token it makes on the forge is removed at the end.
set -euo pipefail

# Each repository, and the issue that makes its legs run on the host's forge.

on_host() {
    local seconds=$1
    shift
    local left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
    [ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 124
    [ "$seconds" -le "$left" ] || seconds=$left
    timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=10 "$seconds" "$REHEARSAL_SSH" "$@"
}

fail() {
    [ -n "$failed" ] || failed="$1: $2"

# Step 1: the workflows on main, as the central forge holds them: no provider named, and every leg asks by kind.
echo "== the workflows"
# shellcheck disable=SC2016
judge='import pathlib, re, sys
for path in sorted(pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).glob(".*/workflows/*.y*ml")):
    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()

        if "Main acceptance" in line:
            call, at = line, number
            while call.rstrip().endswith("\\") and at < len(lines):
                call += " " + lines[at]
                at += 1
            if "--kind" not in call:
                print(f"NOKIND {path.name}:{number}: the leg asks for no kind")'
for repository in "${repositories[@]}"; do
    if ! timeout 600 git clone -q --depth 1 "$forge/$repository.git" "$checkouts/$repository" \
        > "$out/clone-$repository.out" 2>&1; then
        fail "the workflows" "$repository could not be cloned: $(tail -n 1 "$out/clone-$repository.out")"

sed "s/^/$repository: /" "$out/workflows-$repository.out"

    grep -q '^made$' "$out/forge.out" && token_made=yes
    cat "$out/forge.out"
    fail "the legs on the host's forge" "the forge could not be read: $(tail -n 1 "$out/forge.out")"
fi
# The legs of the newest run on main: every one there, each a success.
legs='import json, re, sys
tasks = (json.load(open(sys.argv[1])) or {}).get("workflow_runs") or []
legs = [t for t in tasks if t.get("head_branch") == "main" and re.match(r"(Leg|Acceptance)\b", t.get("name", ""))]
if not legs:
    print("NONE no leg ran on main")
    sys.exit(0)
newest = max(t.get("run_number", 0) for t in legs)

    > "$out/forge-$repository.json" 2> /dev/null || echo '{}' > "$out/forge-$repository.json"
python3 -I -c "$legs" "$out/forge-$repository.json" > "$out/forge-$repository.out" 2>&1 \
    || echo "NONE the forge's answer is no JSON" > "$out/forge-$repository.out"
sed "s/^/$repository on the forge: /" "$out/forge-$repository.out"
if grep -qE '^(NONE|BAD) ' "$out/forge-$repository.out"; then
    wait_on "${issue[$repository]}" "$repository's legs on the host's forge: $(grep -m 1 -E '^(NONE|BAD) ' \

# Step 3: on GitHub, the same workflow's newest legs on main, read as anyone may.
echo "== the legs on GitHub"
github=${forge#https://github.com/}
for repository in "${repositories[@]}"; do
    runs="https://api.github.com/repos/$github/$repository/actions/workflows/build.yml/runs"

good = j.get("conclusion") in ("success", "skipped")

        fail "the legs on GitHub" \
            "$repository: $(grep -m 1 -E '^(BAD|NONE) ' "$out/github-$repository.out" | cut -d' ' -f2-)"
    fi
done

on_host() {
    local seconds=$1
    shift
    local left=$((REHEARSAL_DEADLINE - $(date +%s) - 60))
    [ "$left" -gt 0 ] || return 124
    [ "$seconds" -le "$left" ] || seconds=$left
    timeout --signal=TERM --kill-after=10 "$seconds" "$REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH" "$@"
}

    [ -n "$failed" ] || failed="$1: $2"
    echo "FAILED $1: $2"
}

cleanup() {
    # shellcheck disable=SC2016
    on_host 60 sh -c 'rm -rf "$1"; [ "$2" != yes ] || rm -rf "$HOME/$3"' sh "$scratch" "$placed" "$parent_dir" \
        < /dev/null > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
}

if not answer.get("draft"):
    print("FAIL no project.yml draft")

    fi

#!/bin/sh
# The tasks step: as the test person, three pi tasks on the project's repository with the openrouter test key. Two run
# on, so the room shows displays that share a start (`Agent <Name>` and `Agent <Name> (<task>)`), and answer what is
# meant for them; the third is stopped, so its account has left the room before the busy-room step.
set -eu
key=${REHEARSAL_PROVIDER_KEYS:-}/openrouter-api.txt
if [ -z "${REHEARSAL_PROVIDER_KEYS:-}" ] || [ ! -r "$key" ]; then echo 'no openrouter test key given'; exit 77; fi
# The model the adapters' own builds run pi with on openrouter: cheap, and known to drive pi's tools.

# The key travels on standard input alone; the answer names the entry, never the value.
bounded 120 "$REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH" porelo vault put openrouter < "$key"

# The image is built once, for pi, before three starts would each build it.
bounded 1800 "$REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH" porelo task prepare --agent=pi -p "$REHEARSAL_PROJECT" -r "$REHEARSAL_REPOSITORY" \
    < /dev/null
echo "the pi task image of $REHEARSAL_PROJECT/$REHEARSAL_REPOSITORY is prepared"

another agent. Change no file and push nothing. After $answer_minutes minutes, stop."

host_start() { # <task> [<start option>...]
    task=$1
    shift
    bounded 600 "$REHEARSAL_ORG" task start --host "$REHEARSAL_PERSON@$REHEARSAL_HOST" --no-input --agent=pi \
        --provider=openrouter --model="$model" --clearance=deny -p "$REHEARSAL_PROJECT" -r "$REHEARSAL_REPOSITORY" \

        --host "$REHEARSAL_PERSON@$REHEARSAL_HOST" --no-input --agent=pi --provider=openrouter --model="$model" \
        --clearance=deny -p "$REHEARSAL_PROJECT" -r "$REHEARSAL_REPOSITORY" --minutes=$((answer_minutes + 5)) \
        -P "$prompt" "rehearsal-$name" < /dev/null > "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-$name.start" 2>&1 &
    clients="$clients $!"
done

state() { # <task>: true or false as porelo's status says it runs; anything else is no answer and fails the step
    # A start still under way has made no task yet: that is "not running", which the caller waits on.

start_log() { # <task>: what its start said, for a failure
    echo "its start said:"
    cat "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-$1.start" || true

    done
    echo "rehearsal-$name runs"
done

bounded 60 "$REHEARSAL_PERSON_SSH" porelo task list < /dev/null > "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks.list"
cat "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks.list"
for name in a b c; do
    grep -q "rehearsal-$name" "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks.list" || { echo "task list does not name rehearsal-$name"; exit 1; }
done
for name in a b; do
    now=$(state "$name") || exit 1
    [ "$now" = true ] || { echo "rehearsal-$name stopped on its own"; start_log "$name"; exit 1; }

[ -n "$own" ] || { echo "the start of $all_task names no own repository of $REHEARSAL_PROJECT"; exit 1; }
expected=$(python3 -I -c 'import json, sys
for each in json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["repositories"]["names"]:
    print(each["name"])
print(sys.argv[2])' "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-all.project.json" "$own") || { echo "no repositories in porelo project show"; exit 1; }
[ -n "$expected" ] || { echo "porelo project show names no repository of $REHEARSAL_PROJECT"; exit 1; }
in_task ls -1 /workspace > "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-all.workspace" \
    || { echo "/workspace of $all_task cannot be listed"; exit 1; }
for repository in $expected; do

    || { echo "a repository under /workspace has a git remote"; exit 1; }
in_task env | sed 's/=.*//' > "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-all.env-names"
! grep -qiE 'gate|push' "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-all.env-names" \
    || { echo "$all_task holds a variable for a gate or a push: $(grep -iE 'gate|push' "$REHEARSAL_REPORT/tasks-all.env-names" | tr '\n' ' ')"; exit 1; }

[ -z "$left_over" ] || { echo "$all_task left behind: $(echo "$left_over" | tr '\n' ' ')"; exit 1; }
echo "$all_task is gone: no task, container, volume or workspace of it is left"

# Exits 0 when every scenario is OK, 77 (SKIP, the last line says why) when the VM, the checkout or the time is
# missing, and 1 for a failed rehearsal or a contract value missing. The report goes to REHEARSAL_REPORT/window.d.
# The test person's key reaches the run only through the agent REHEARSAL_PERSON_AGENT names, for that one call.
# The central token reaches it only as REHEARSAL_CENTRAL_TOKEN, the path of the program that prints it, never the token.

for name in REHEARSAL_HOST REHEARSAL_HOST_NAME REHEARSAL_HOST_KEY REHEARSAL_PERSON REHEARSAL_PERSON_AGENT \
    REHEARSAL_CENTRAL_TOKEN REHEARSAL_PROJECT REHEARSAL_REPORT REHEARSAL_DEADLINE; do
    [ -n "${!name:-}" ] || {
        echo "window: $name is not set"

for name in REHEARSAL_WINDOW_VM REHEARSAL_WINDOW_VM_KEY REHEARSAL_WINDOW_CHECKOUT; do
    [ -n "${!name:-}" ] || skip "$name is not set"
done
rehearse=$REHEARSAL_WINDOW_CHECKOUT/tool/rehearse_window.sh
[ -x "$rehearse" ] || skip "the checkout has no executable tool/rehearse_window.sh"
vm=(--vm "$REHEARSAL_WINDOW_VM" --vm-key "$REHEARSAL_WINDOW_VM_KEY")

[ "$budget" -ge 10 ] || skip "only $budget minutes left for the window, fewer than 10"

[ "$status" -ne 124 ] && [ "$status" -ne 137 ] || echo "window: stopped at the deadline"

#!/bin/sh
# Runs ansible-test sanity on the collection in the tools image build/tools-image pins, with sanity's requirements
# installed by hash from build/sanity/requirements.txt, and no network while it runs; the same here and in the Build.
# usage: build/sanity.sh [<ansible-test sanity argument>...]
set -eu
root=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
image=$(cat "$root/build/tools-image")
# Taken by digest only: a tag alone could be moved to another image under the same name.
printf '%s\n' "$image" | grep -Eqx '[a-z0-9./-]+:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}' \
    || { echo "REFUSED: build/tools-image names no image by digest: $image" >&2; exit 1; }
command -v podman >/dev/null || { echo "REFUSED: podman is not installed, and it runs the sanity" >&2; exit 1; }
podman build -q --pull=missing --build-arg "TOOLS_IMAGE=$image" -t localhost/porelo-host-sanity "$root/build/sanity" \

tar -C "$work" -xf -
cp /opt/sanity/get_pip_25_2.py "$HOME/.ansible/test/cache/"
cd "$work"
exec ansible-test sanity --local --python 3.14 --color no "$@"' sanity "$@"

#!/bin/sh
# Stage one checked local package set before a VM or a remote host is changed.
set -eu
fail() { printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
valid() {
    case $1 in *'
'*) return 1 ;; esac
    printf '%s\n' "$1" | grep -Eqx "$2"

mkdir -m 700 -- "$dest"
finish() {
    status=$?
    trap - EXIT HUP INT TERM
    if [ "$status" != 0 ]; then rm -rf -- "$dest"; fi
    exit "$status"
}
trap finish EXIT
trap 'exit 130' HUP INT TERM
cp -- "$host" "$dest/porelo-host.deb"

#!/bin/sh
# Runs one of the Ansible tools from the image build/tools-image pins, on this checkout mounted where ansible-galaxy
# expects a collection, as the account that calls it; the same here and in the Build.
# usage: build/tools.sh <command> [<argument>...]    e.g. build/tools.sh ansible-lint -c build/ansible-lint.yml
set -eu
[ $# -ge 1 ] || { echo "usage: $0 <command> [<argument>...]" >&2; exit 2; }
root=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
image=$(cat "$root/build/tools-image")
# Taken by digest only: a tag alone could be moved to another image under the same name.
printf '%s\n' "$image" | grep -Eqx '[a-z0-9./-]+:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}' \
    || { echo "REFUSED: build/tools-image names no image by digest: $image" >&2; exit 1; }
# Inside that image already, as a Porelo task built from porelo.yml is, the command runs here, with the checkout linked

fi
command -v podman >/dev/null || { echo "REFUSED: podman is not installed, and it runs the tools" >&2; exit 1; }
work=/work/ansible_collections/porelo/host
# Only this collection's own variables pass in, so nothing else of the caller's environment reaches the tools.

-v "$root:$work:Z" -w "$work" "$image" "$@"

    [ ! -e "$path" ] || { echo "earlier run left $path" >&2; exit 1; }
done
porelo --help
printf 'a leg\n' > "$HOME/left-by-pool-leg"
echo 'pool acceptance leg passed'

# OS specific support.
native_path() { printf %s\\n "$1"; }
case "$(uname)" in
CYGWIN* | MINGW*)
  [ -z "${JAVA_HOME-}" ] || JAVA_HOME="$(cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME")"
  native_path() { cygpath --path --windows "$1"; }
  ;;

# For Cygwin and MinGW, ensure paths are in Unix format before anything is touched
if [ -n "${JAVA_HOME-}" ]; then
  if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ]; then

if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] || [ ! -x "$JAVACCMD" ]; then
  echo "The JAVA_HOME environment variable is not defined correctly, so mvnw cannot run." >&2
  echo "JAVA_HOME is set to \"$JAVA_HOME\", but \"\$JAVA_HOME/bin/java\" or \"\$JAVA_HOME/bin/javac\" does not exist." >&2
  return 1
fi

# hash string like Java String::hashCode

verbose() { :; }
[ "${MVNW_VERBOSE-}" != true ] || verbose() { printf %s\\n "${1-}"; }

trim() {
  # MWRAPPER-139:
  #   Trims trailing and leading whitespace, carriage returns, tabs, and linefeeds.

scriptDir="$(dirname "$0")"
scriptName="$(basename "$0")"

:Darwin*x86_64) distributionPlatform=darwin-amd64 ;;
:Darwin*arm64) distributionPlatform=darwin-aarch64 ;;

# apply MVNW_REPOURL and calculate MAVEN_HOME
# maven home pattern: ~/.m2/wrapper/dists/{apache-maven-<version>,maven-mvnd-<version>-<platform>}/<hash>
[ -z "${MVNW_REPOURL-}" ] || distributionUrl="$MVNW_REPOURL$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN${distributionUrl#*"$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN"}"
distributionUrlName="${distributionUrl##*/}"

exec_maven() {
  unset MVNW_VERBOSE MVNW_USERNAME MVNW_PASSWORD MVNW_REPOURL || :
  exec "$MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD" "$@" || die "cannot exec $MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD"
}

  clean() { rm -rf -- "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR"; }
  trap clean HUP INT TERM EXIT
else
  die "cannot create temp dir"

# Download and Install Apache Maven
verbose "Couldn't find MAVEN_HOME, downloading and installing it ..."
verbose "Downloading from: $distributionUrl"
verbose "Downloading to: $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName"

  distributionUrl="${distributionUrl%.zip}.tar.gz"
  distributionUrlName="${distributionUrl##*/}"
fi

verbose "Found wget ... using wget"
wget ${__MVNW_QUIET_WGET:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_WGET"} "$distributionUrl" -O "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" || die "wget: Failed to fetch $distributionUrl"

public class Downloader extends java.net.Authenticator
{
  protected java.net.PasswordAuthentication getPasswordAuthentication()

    }
  }
  END
# For Cygwin/MinGW, switch paths to Windows format before running javac and java
verbose " - Compiling Downloader.java ..."
"$(native_path "$JAVACCMD")" "$(native_path "$javaSource")" || die "Failed to compile Downloader.java"
verbose " - Running Downloader.java ..."

  else
    echo "Checksum validation was requested but neither 'sha256sum' or 'shasum' are available." >&2
    echo "Please install either command, or disable validation by removing 'distributionSha256Sum' from your maven-wrapper.properties." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  if [ $distributionSha256Result = false ]; then
    echo "Error: Failed to validate Maven distribution SHA-256, your Maven distribution might be compromised." >&2
    echo "If you updated your Maven version, you need to update the specified distributionSha256Sum property." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
fi

# Find the actual extracted directory name (handles snapshots where filename != directory name)
actualDistributionDir=""

# If not found, search for any directory with the Maven executable (for snapshots)
if [ -z "$actualDistributionDir" ]; then
  # enable globbing to iterate over items

mv -- "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$actualDistributionDir" "$MAVEN_HOME" || [ -d "$MAVEN_HOME" ] || die "fail to move MAVEN_HOME"

# Gives this pool VM's run account back: the one command the account may run as root through sudo, and it takes no
# argument. It writes 'resetting' and powers the VM off; the host, outside every job, sees it stopped, reverts it to
# its base snapshot - taken with 'ready' written - and starts it, so nothing a run did reaches the next.
# porelo-machines waits until /var/lib/porelo-pool/<account> says 'ready' again. Shell, since it runs inside a pool
# VM, which has no Java.
set -eu
[ $# -eq 0 ] || { echo "porelo-pool-give-back takes no argument" >&2; exit 2; }
account=${SUDO_USER:-}
[ -n "$account" ] || { echo "porelo-pool-give-back runs through sudo, as the run account" >&2; exit 2; }
[ -f "/var/lib/porelo-pool/$account" ] || { echo "'$account' is no run account of this pool VM" >&2; exit 2; }
printf 'resetting\n' > "/var/lib/porelo-pool/$account"
# Two seconds later, so this answer reaches the caller before the VM goes down.

# shellcheck shell=bash
# The one rule by which a pinned download is tried again, sourced by the steps of this action and by any script that
# fetches something pinned (shfmt, the wrapper's Maven, fd, ripgrep, Node): the same numbers as porelo-pinned-rule's
# PinnedRetry, which a test in porelo-machines holds this file to. The pauses double from 15 s, none longer than 5 min,
# until 15 min of pauses are spent, the last one cut to fit: 15 30 60 120 240 300 135, eight attempts in all. GitHub's
# release downloads answered 504 for over ten minutes on 2026-11-08.

    [ "$spent" -lt 900 ] || return 1
    [ $((spent + next)) -le 900 ] || next=$((900 - spent))
    [ "$next" -le "$left" ] || next=$left
    echo "$next"
}

pinned_sha256() { # <file>: its sha256

pinned_fetch() { # <url> <sha256> <file>: the file the url serves, checked, or the mirror's copy of it when the url
    # fails; one already there that matches is kept
    local url=$1 sha256=$2 file=$3 mirror=${PORELO_PINNED_MIRROR-$pinned_mirror} name second= start status=0
    if [ -n "$mirror" ] && [[ ! $mirror =~ ^https://[^][:space:]@?#[]+$ ]]; then
        # Not echoed: a user or a query in the address could carry a secret into the log.
        echo "::error::PORELO_PINNED_MIRROR is no https:// address without whitespace, a user, a query or a fragment" >&2
        return 1
    fi
    # A link is no cached file: it would hand over whatever it points at.
    if [ -f "$file" ] && [ ! -L "$file" ] && [ "$(pinned_sha256 "$file")" = "$sha256" ]; then
        echo "$file taken from the cache, checked against $sha256" >&2
        return 0

pinned_from mirror "$second" "$sha256" "$file" "$start" || status=$?

pinned_from() { # <only|first|mirror> <url> <sha256> <file> <start>: one source tried by the rule until its deadline;
    # 0 when the file is in place, 1 on a fault no other source mends, 2 when this source did not serve it
    local role=$1 url=$2 sha256=$3 file=$4 start=$5 attempt=1 deadline=900 level=error part code exit wait elapsed \

        deadline=$pinned_first_deadline
        level=warning
        ;;
esac
while :; do

rm -f "$part"
echo "::error::$url has the digest of something else, not the pinned $sha256" >&2
return 1

        ;;
esac
rm -f "$part"
elapsed=$(($(pinned_now) - start))
if ! wait=$(pinned_pause "$attempt" "$elapsed" "$deadline"); then

# For a fetch the script does not make itself and whose failure does not say why, such as the wrapper's Maven: every
# failure is tried again, by the same pauses. The command runs in a subshell of its own under set -e, outside any
# condition, so a function given stops at its first failing command; the caller's errexit is left as it was. Called as
# a condition itself (`if pinned_retry ...`, `pinned_retry ... || ...`), bash ignores set -e all the way down, so a
# function given there must chain its own steps with &&.
pinned_retry() { # <what> <command>...: runs the command until it succeeds
    local what=$1 attempt=1 wait start elapsed status errexit=
    shift
    start=$(pinned_now) || return 1
    case $- in *e*) errexit=1 ;; esac

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Installs the musl cross-toolchain plus a musl-built zlib, as required by

PREFIX="${MUSL_PREFIX:-$HOME/.local/opt}"
TC="$PREFIX/x86_64-linux-musl-native"
WORK="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$WORK"' EXIT

# every network - GitHub's hosted runners cannot connect to it at all - so a mirror has to be
# usable. The digests are not: the digest is what is trusted, not the host, so it is written here
# and nothing in the environment can replace it - a mirror serves these bytes or the install stops.
MUSL_URL="${MUSL_URL:-https://musl.cc/x86_64-linux-musl-native.tgz}"
MUSL_SHA256=eb1db6f0f3c2bdbdbfb993d7ef7e2eeef82ac1259f6a6e1757c33a97dbcef3ad
ZLIB_URL="${ZLIB_URL:-https://zlib.net/fossils/zlib-$ZLIB_VERSION.tar.gz}"
ZLIB_SHA256=9a93b2b7dfdac77ceba5a558a580e74667dd6fede4585b91eefb60f03b72df23

echo "==> downloading musl toolchain"
curl -fSL --retry 3 -o "$WORK/musl.tgz" "$MUSL_URL"

export PATH="$TC/bin:$PATH"

if [ ! -f "$TC/lib/libz.a" ]; then
    echo "==> building zlib $ZLIB_VERSION against musl"
    curl -fSL --retry 3 -o "$WORK/zlib.tar.gz" "$ZLIB_URL"
    verify "$WORK/zlib.tar.gz" "$ZLIB_SHA256"
    tar -xzf "$WORK/zlib.tar.gz" -C "$WORK"
    cd "$WORK/zlib-$ZLIB_VERSION"
    CC=x86_64-linux-musl-gcc ./configure --static --prefix="$TC"

# Optional ENV vars
# -----------------
#   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir, required when download maven via java source

# set JAVACMD and JAVACCMD
set_java_home() {
  # For Cygwin and MinGW, ensure paths are in Unix format before anything is touched
  if [ -n "${JAVA_HOME-}" ]; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ]; then
      # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
      JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
      JAVACCMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/javac"

if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] || [ ! -x "$JAVACCMD" ]; then
  echo "The JAVA_HOME environment variable is not defined correctly, so mvnw cannot run." >&2

)" || :
JAVACCMD="$(
  'set' +e
  'unset' -f command 2>/dev/null
  'command' -v javac
)" || :

die() {
  printf %s\\n "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

  #   exotic environments such as mingw bash on Windows.
  printf "%s" "${1}" | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

# parse distributionUrl and optional distributionSha256Sum, requires .mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties
while IFS="=" read -r key value; do
  case "${key-}" in
  distributionUrl) distributionUrl=$(trim "${value-}") ;;
  distributionSha256Sum) distributionSha256Sum=$(trim "${value-}") ;;
  esac
done <"$scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties"
[ -n "${distributionUrl-}" ] || die "cannot read distributionUrl property in $scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties"

distributionPlatform=linux-amd64
;;

# apply MVNW_REPOURL and calculate MAVEN_HOME
# maven home pattern: ~/.m2/wrapper/dists/{apache-maven-<version>,maven-mvnd-<version>-<platform>}/<hash>
[ -z "${MVNW_REPOURL-}" ] || distributionUrl="$MVNW_REPOURL$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN${distributionUrl#*"$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN"}"

case "${distributionUrl-}" in
*?-bin.zip | *?maven-mvnd-?*-?*.zip) ;;
*) die "distributionUrl is not valid, must match *-bin.zip or maven-mvnd-*.zip, but found '${distributionUrl-}'" ;;
esac

# verbose opt
__MVNW_QUIET_WGET=--quiet __MVNW_QUIET_CURL=--silent __MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP=-q __MVNW_QUIET_TAR=''
[ "${MVNW_VERBOSE-}" != true ] || __MVNW_QUIET_WGET='' __MVNW_QUIET_CURL='' __MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP='' __MVNW_QUIET_TAR=v

if [ -z "${MVNW_USERNAME-}" ] && command -v wget >/dev/null; then
  verbose "Found wget ... using wget"
  wget ${__MVNW_QUIET_WGET:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_WGET"} "$distributionUrl" -O "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" || die "wget: Failed to fetch $distributionUrl"
elif [ -z "${MVNW_USERNAME-}" ] && command -v curl >/dev/null; then
  verbose "Found curl ... using curl"
  curl ${__MVNW_QUIET_CURL:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_CURL"} -f -L -o "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" "$distributionUrl" || die "curl: Failed to fetch $distributionUrl"
elif set_java_home; then
  verbose "Falling back to use Java to download"
  javaSource="$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/Downloader.java"

  setDefault( new Downloader() );
  java.nio.file.Files.copy( java.net.URI.create( args[0] ).toURL().openStream(), java.nio.file.Paths.get( args[1] ).toAbsolutePath().normalize() );
}

  if echo "$distributionSha256Sum  $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" | sha256sum -c - >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    distributionSha256Result=true
  fi
elif command -v shasum >/dev/null; then
  if echo "$distributionSha256Sum  $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" | shasum -a 256 -c >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "If you updated your Maven version, you need to update the specified distributionSha256Sum property." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
fi

actualDistributionDir="$distributionUrlNameMain"

clean || :
exec_maven "$@"
